950 entries, browser-qa to bugs. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **browser-qa**: Read-only browser QA role. Do not inspect app source code unless the master explicitly asks for a targeted implementation clue. ([AnsarUllahAnasZ360/cc-guide](https://github.com/AnsarUllahAnasZ360/cc-guide/blob/HEAD/plugins/proof-driven-verification/agents/browser-qa.md))
- **browser-qa**: Fallback only when Codex fails or has no quota (packet needs a `Codex fallback:` line): Behavior checks in a real browser through scripts (Playwright or the project probe): flows, dialogs, URL state, console errors, keyboard and 390px layout. - **browser-qa**: Behavior checks in a real browser through scripts (Playwright or the project probe): flows, dialogs, URL state, console errors, keyboard and 390px layout. Reports text results; no product edits. ([AO-HyS/development-system](https://github.com/AO-HyS/development-system/blob/HEAD/artifacts/1.39.0/claude/agents/browser-qa.md) · 2 copies)
- **browser-qa**: Use when testing web features end-to-end. Autonomously navigates pages, performs UI interactions, monitors network/console activity, intelligently waits for async operations to complete, and generates detailed QA reports with screenshots and PASS/FAIL judgment. Uses agent-browser CLI for token-efficient browser automation. ([choidabom/devconfig](https://github.com/choidabom/devconfig/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/browser-qa.md))
- **browser-qa**: Verifies changes in the running app — drives the preview browser, checks console and network, tests interactions, captures screenshots as proof. Use after a UI or editor change that a unit test cannot prove. Reports what it observed; does not fix code. ([elchanany/hypescript](https://github.com/elchanany/hypescript/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/browser-qa.md))
- **browser-qa**: Reproduces a reported bug or records the current behavior before a change (PRECHECK, stage 2), and re-runs the same steps plus acceptance cases on the local build after the change (POSTCHECK, stage 7). Uses only the browser on configured URLs and records evidence. Never edits code. Called by the orchestrator. ([fady-wafaa/engineering-ops](https://github.com/fady-wafaa/engineering-ops/blob/HEAD/agents/browser-qa.md))
- **browser-qa**: 輕量網頁 QA，只處理不需要真實登入的頁面。用於本機 dev server 或靜態頁的視覺檢查、RWD 斷點檢查、console 錯誤檢查、確認 SPA 路由能正常顯示。只診斷、絕不改 code。使用內建 Browser pane 工具（需要 Claude Code 桌面版的內建瀏覽器；沒有就無法使用）。 ([fireman333/Klaude-Package-For-Share](https://github.com/fireman333/Klaude-Package-For-Share/blob/HEAD/plugins/klaude-productivity/agents/browser-qa.md))
- **browser-qa**: Visual QA in a real browser. Opens the app, interacts with it (navigate, click, fill test data, keyboard), takes screenshots, and judges each check in the brief visually — PASS/FAIL per check with screenshot paths. Local dev hosts only unless the brief names a staging URL. Use after a UI change, before claiming it works. Never for production sites, real credentials, or real payments. ([gmorel/efficient-claude-harness](https://github.com/gmorel/efficient-claude-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/ech/agents/browser-qa.md))

- **brvm-stratege**: Agent d'analyse stratégique BRVM. S'appuie sur les données live fournies par l'agent brvm-marche. À utiliser pour : analyser une valeur avant achat, évaluer si le moment est opportun, comparer plusieurs valeurs, vérifier la cohérence d'un portefeuille. ([elysee15/financial-analyst](https://github.com/elysee15/financial-analyst/blob/HEAD/agents/stratege.md))

DO NOT USE FOR: a verdict, a fix, or a file list. `bug-verifier` judges, a fix session fixes, and the caller supplies the list.
